<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum ko Abul Yaman Hakam bin Nafi ne hadith bayan ki, unhein is hadith ki Shuaib ne khabar di. Unhone Zuhri se yeh hadith suni. Unhein Ubaidullah Ibn-e-Abdullah Ibn-e-Utba bin Masood ne khabar di ke Abdullah bin Abbas se Abu Sufyan bin Harb ne yeh waqiya bayan kiya ke Heraclius (Shah-e-Room) ne unke paas Quraish ke qafle mein ek aadmi bulane ko bheja aur us waqt yeh log tijarat ke liye mulk-e-Sham gaye hue the aur yeh woh zamana tha jab Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Quraish aur Abu Sufyan se ek waqti ahd kiya hua tha. Jab Abu Sufyan aur dusre log Heraclius ke paas Iliya pohanche jahan Heraclius ne darbar talab kiya tha. Uske gird Room ke baray baray log (ulama, wuzara, umara) baithe hue the. Heraclius ne unko aur apne tarjuman ko bulwaya. Phir unse pucha ke tum mein se kaun shakhs mudda&#x27;i-e-risalat ka zyada qareebi azeez hai? Abu Sufyan kehte hain ke main bol utha ke main uska sab se zyada qareebi rishtedar hoon. (Yeh sun kar) Heraclius ne hukum diya ke isko (Abu Sufyan ko) mere qareeb la kar bithao aur uske sathiyon ko uski peeth ke peeche bitha do. Phir apne tarjuman se kaha ke in logon se keh do ke main Abu Sufyan se is shakhs ke (yani Muhammad Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ke) halat puchta hoon. Agar yeh mujh se kisi baat mein jhoot bol de to tum uska jhoot zahir kar dena, (Abu Sufyan ka qaul hai ke) Allah ki qasam! Agar mujhe yeh ghairat na aati ke yeh log mujh ko jhutlayenge to main aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki nisbat zaroor ghalat-goi se kaam leta. Khair pehli baat jo Heraclius ne mujh se puchi woh yeh ke is shakhs ka khandan tum logon mein kaisa hai? Main ne kaha woh to baray unchay aali nasab wale hain. Kehne laga is se pehle bhi kisi ne tum logon mein aisi baat kahi thi? Main ne kaha nahi. Kehne laga, acha uske baron mein koi badshah hua hai? Main ne kaha nahi. Phir usne kaha, baray logon ne uski pairwi ikhtiyar ki hai ya kamzoron ne? Main ne kaha nahi kamzoron ne. Phir kehne laga, uske tabedar roz barhte jate hain ya koi sathi phir bhi jata hai? Main ne kaha nahi. Kehne laga ke kya apne is da&#x27;way (nabuwat) se pehle kabhi (kisi bhi mauqe par) usne jhoot bola hai? Main ne kaha nahi. Aur ab hamari us se (sulh ki) ek muqarrara muddat thehri hui hai. Maloom nahi woh is mein kya karne wala hai. (Abu Sufyan kehte hain) main is baat ke siwa aur koi (jhoot) is guftagu mein shamil na kar saka. Heraclius ne kaha kya tumhari us se kabhi larayi bhi hoti hai? Hum ne kaha ke han. Bola phir tumhari aur uski jang ka kya haal hota hai? Main ne kaha, larayi dol ki tarah hai, kabhi woh hum se (maidan-e-jang) jeet lete hain aur kabhi hum un se jeet lete hain. Heraclius ne pucha. Woh tumhein kis baat ka hukum deta hai? Main ne kaha woh kehta hai ke sirf ek Allah hi ki ibadat karo, uska kisi ko shareek na banao aur apne baap dada ki (shirk ki) baatein chor do aur hamein namaz parhne, sach bolne, parhezgari aur sila-rehmi ka hukum deta hai. (Yeh sab sun kar) phir Heraclius ne apne tarjuman se kaha ke Abu Sufyan se keh de ke main ne tum se uska nasab pucha to tum ne kaha ke woh hum mein aali nasab hai aur paighambar apni qaum mein aali nasab hi bheje jaya karte hain. Main ne tum se pucha ke (da&#x27;wa-e-nabuwat ki) yeh baat tumhare andar is se pehle kisi aur ne bhi kahi thi, to tum ne jawab diya ke nahi, tab main ne (apne dil mein) kaha ke agar yeh baat is se pehle kisi ne kahi hoti to main samajhta ke is shakhs ne bhi isi baat ki taqleed ki hai jo pehle kahi ja chuki hai. Main ne tum se pucha ke uske baron mein koi badshah bhi guzra hai, tum ne kaha ke nahi. To main ne (dil mein) kaha ke unke buzurgon mein se koi badshah hua hoga to keh dunga ke woh shakhs (is bahane) apne aaba o ajdad ki badshahat aur unka mulk (dobara) hasil karna chahta hai. Aur main ne tum se pucha ke is baat ke kehne (yani paighambari ka da&#x27;wa karne) se pehle tum ne kabhi usko darogh-goi ka ilzam lagaya hai? Tum ne kaha ke nahi. To main ne samajh liya ke jo shakhs admiyon ke sath darogh-goi se bache woh Allah ke bare mein kaise jhooti baat keh sakta hai. Aur main ne tum se pucha ke baray log uske pairu hote hain ya kamzor aadmi. Tum ne kaha kamzoron ne uski itteba ki hai, to (dar-asal) yahi log paighambaron ke muttaba&#x27;een hote hain. Aur main ne tum se pucha ke uske sathi barh rahe hain ya kam ho rahe hain. Tum ne kaha ke woh barh rahe hain aur iman ki kaifiyat yahi hoti hai. Hatta ke woh kamil ho jata hai aur main ne tum se pucha ke aaya koi shakhs uske deen se na-khush ho kar murtad bhi ho jata hai tum ne kaha nahi, to iman ki khasiyat bhi yahi hai jin ke dilon mein uski musarrat rach bas jaye woh us se lauta nahi karte. Aur main ne tum se pucha ke aaya woh kabhi ahd-shikni karte hain. Tum ne kaha nahi, paighambaron ka yahi haal hota hai, woh ahd ki khilaf-warzi nahi karte. Aur main ne tum se kaha ke woh tum se kis cheez ke liye kehte hain. Tum ne kaha ke woh hamein hukum dete hain ke Allah ki ibadat karo, uske sath kisi ko shareek na thehrao aur tumhein buton ki parastish se rokte hain. Sach bolne aur parhezgari ka hukum dete hain. Lehaza agar yeh baatein jo tum keh rahe ho sach hain to anqareeb woh is jagah ka malik ho jayega ke jahan mere yeh dono paon hain. Mujhe maloom tha ke woh (paighambar) aane wala hai. Magar mujhe yeh maloom nahi tha ke woh tumhare andar hoga. Agar main janta ke us tak pohanch sakunga to us se milne ke liye har takleef gawara karta. Agar main uske paas hota to uske paon dhota. Heraclius ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) woh khat mangaya jo aapne Dihya Kalbi (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke zariye hakim-e-Basra ke paas bheja tha aur usne woh Heraclius ke paas bhej diya tha. Phir usko parha to us mein (likha tha): Allah ke naam ke sath jo nihayat meharban aur raham wala hai. Allah ke bande aur uske paighambar Muhammad (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki taraf se yeh khat hai Shah-e-Room ke liye. Is shakhs par salam ho jo hidayat ki pairwi kare iske baad main aapke samne da&#x27;wat-e-Islam pesh karta hoon. Agar aap Islam le aayenge to (deen o duniya mein) salamti naseeb hogi. Allah aapko dohra sawab dega aur agar aap (meri da&#x27;wat se) ro-gardani karenge to aapki ra&#x27;aya ka gunah bhi aap hi par hoga. Aur ae Ahl-e-Kitab! Ek aisi baat par aa jao jo hamare aur tumhare darmiyan yaksan hai. Woh yeh ke hum Allah ke siwa kisi ki ibadat na karein aur kisi ko uska shareek na thehrayein aur na hum mein se koi kisi ko Allah ke siwa apna Rab banaye. Phir agar woh Ahl-e-Kitab (is baat se) munh pher lein to (musalmano!) tum un se keh do ke (tum mano ya na mano) hum to ek Allah ke itaat-guzar hain. Abu Sufyan kehte hain: Jab Heraclius ne jo kuch kehna tha keh diya aur khat parh kar farigh hua to uske ird-gird bahut shor o ghaugha hua, bahut si awazein uthin aur hamein bahar nikal diya gaya. Tab main ne apne sathiyon se kaha ke Abu Kabsha ke bete (Nabi Kareem Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ka mamla to bahut barh gaya (dekho to) is se Bani Asfar (Room) ka badshah bhi darta hai. Mujhe us waqt se is baat ka yaqeen ho gaya ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) anqareeb ghalib ho kar rahenge. Hatta ke Allah ne mujhe musalman kar diya. (Rawi ka bayan hai ke) Ibn-e-Natoor Iliya ka hakim Heraclius ka musahib aur Sham ke Nasara ka laat padri bayan karta tha ke Heraclius jab Iliya aaya, ek din subah ko pareshan utha to uske darbariyon ne daryaft kiya ke aaj hum aapki halat badli hui pate hain. (Kya wajah hai?) Ibn-e-Natoor ka bayan hai ke Heraclius najumi tha, ilm-e-nujoom mein woh puri maharat rakhta tha. Usne apne ham-nasheenon ko bataya ke main ne aaj raat sitaron par nazar dali to dekha ke khatna karne walon ka badshah hamare mulk par ghalib aa gaya hai. (Bhala) is zamane mein kaun log khatna karte hain? Unhone kaha ke Yahood ke siwa koi khatna nahi karta. So unki wajah se pareshan na hon. Saltanat ke tamam sheharon mein yeh hukum likh bhejiye ke wahan jitne Yahudi hon sab qatl kar diye jayein. Woh log inhi baton mein mashghool the ke Heraclius ke paas ek aadmi laya gaya. Jise Shah-e-Ghassan ne bheja tha. Usne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke halat bayan kiye. Jab Heraclius ne (sare halat) sun liye to kaha ke ja kar dekho woh khatna kiye hue hai ya nahi? Unhone use dekha to batlaya ke woh khatna kiya hua hai. Heraclius ne jab us shakhs se Arab ke bare mein pucha to usne batlaya ke woh khatna karte hain. Tab Heraclius ne kaha ke yeh hi (Muhammad Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) is ummat ke badshah hain jo paida ho chuke hain. Phir usne apne ek dost ko Rumiya khat likha aur woh bhi ilm-e-nujoom mein Heraclius ki tarah mahir tha. Phir wahan se Heraclius Homs chala gaya. Abhi Homs se nikla nahi tha ke uske dost ka khat (uske jawab mein) aa gaya. Uski raye bhi Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zuhoor ke bare mein Heraclius ke muwafiq thi ke Muhammad (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) (waqayi) paighambar hain. Iske baad Heraclius ne Room ke baray admiyon ko apne Homs ke mahal mein talab kiya aur uske hukum se mahal ke darwaze band kar liye gaye. Phir woh (apne khas mahal se) bahar aaya aur kaha &quot;Ae Room walo! Kya hidayat aur kamyabi mein kuch hissa tumhare liye bhi hai? Agar tum apni saltanat ki baqa chahte ho to phir is Nabi ki bai&#x27;at kar lo aur musalman ho jao&quot; (Yeh sunna tha ke) phir woh log wahshi gadhon ki tarah darwazon ki taraf dore (magar) unhein band paya. Aakhir jab Heraclius ne (is baat se) unki yeh nafrat dekhi aur unke iman lane se mayus ho gaya to kehne laga ke in logon ko mere paas lao. (Jab woh dobara aaye) to usne kaha main ne jo baat kahi thi us se tumhari deeni pukhtagi ki aazmaish maqsood thi so woh main ne dekh li. Tab (yeh baat sun kar) woh sab ke sab uske samne sajde mein gir pare aur us se khush ho gaye. Bil-aakhir Heraclius ki aakhri halat yeh hi rahi. Abu Abdullah kehte hain ke is hadith ko Saleh bin Kaisan, Yunus aur Ma&#x27;mar ne bhi Zuhri se riwayat kiya hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 7]</p>

## Narration 8

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A8)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مُوسَى، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنَا حَنْظَلَةُ بْنُ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ بْنِ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ ـ رضى الله عنهما ـ قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ بُنِيَ الإِسْلاَمُ عَلَى خَمْسٍ شَهَادَةِ أَنْ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَأَنَّ مُحَمَّدًا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ، وَإِقَامِ الصَّلاَةِ، وَإِيتَاءِ الزَّكَاةِ، وَالْحَجِّ، وَصَوْمِ رَمَضَانَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 8]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/8" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Ibn &#x27;Umar: Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: Islam is based on (the following) five (principles): 1. To testify that none has the right to be worshipped but Allah and Muhammad is Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him). 2. To offer the (compulsory congregational) prayers dutifully and perfectly. 3. To pay Zakat (i.e. obligatory charity) . 4. To perform Hajj. (i.e. Pilgrimage to Mecca) 5. To observe fast during the month of Ramadan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 8]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत इब्न उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) से रिवायत है कि रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: इस्लाम की बुनियाद पाँच चीज़ों पर है: इस बात की गवाही देना कि अल्लाह के सिवा कोई मअ़बूद नहीं और मुहम्मद (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अल्लाह के रसूल हैं, नमाज़ क़ाइम करना, ज़कात देना, हज करना और रमज़ान के रोज़े रखना।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 8]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اسلام کی بنیاد پانچ چیزوں پر ہے: اس بات کی گواہی دینا کہ اللہ کے سوا کوئی معبود نہیں اور محمد صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اللہ کے رسول ہیں، نماز قائم کرنا، زکوٰۃ دینا، حج کرنا اور رمضان کے روزے رکھنا۔</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ibn Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Islam ki bunyaad paanch cheezon par hai: is baat ki gawahee dena ke Allah ke siwa koi mabood nahi aur Muhammad (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Allah ke Rasool hain, namaz qaim karna, zakat dena, Hajj karna aur Ramadan ke roze rakhna.</p>

## Narration 9

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A9)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو عَامِرٍ الْعَقَدِيُّ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ بِلاَلٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الإِيمَانُ بِضْعٌ وَسِتُّونَ شُعْبَةً، وَالْحَيَاءُ شُعْبَةٌ مِنَ الإِيمَانِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 9]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Huraira: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Faith (Belief) consists of more than sixty branches (i.e. parts). And Haya (This term &quot;Haya&quot; covers a large number of concepts which are to be taken together; amongst them are self respect, modesty, bashfulness, and scruple, etc.) is a part of faith</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 9]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ईमान की साठ से कुछ ज़्यादा शाख़ें हैं और हया ईमान की एक शाख़ है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 9]</p>

## Narration 10

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A10)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا آدَمُ بْنُ أَبِي إِيَاسٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي السَّفَرِ، وَإِسْمَاعِيلَ، عَنِ الشَّعْبِيِّ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو ـ رضى الله عنهما ـ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الْمُسْلِمُ مَنْ سَلِمَ الْمُسْلِمُونَ مِنْ لِسَانِهِ وَيَدِهِ، وَالْمُهَاجِرُ مَنْ هَجَرَ مَا نَهَى اللَّهُ عَنْهُ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو عَبْدِ اللَّهِ وَقَالَ أَبُو مُعَاوِيَةَ حَدَّثَنَا دَاوُدُ عَنْ عَامِرٍ قَالَ سَمِعْتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم‏.‏ وَقَالَ عَبْدُ الأَعْلَى عَنْ دَاوُدَ عَنْ عَامِرٍ عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 10]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/10" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated &#x27;Abdullah bin &#x27;Amr: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;A Muslim is the one who avoids harming Muslims with his tongue and hands. And a Muhajir (emigrant) is the one who gives up (abandons) all what Allah has forbidden</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 10]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/10" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   10 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अब्दुल्लाह बिन अम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) से रिवायत है कि नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मुसलमान वह है जिस की ज़बान और हाथ से दूसरे मुसलमान महफ़ूज़ रहें, और मुहाजिर वह है जो उन चीज़ों को छोड़ दे जिन से अल्लाह ने मना किया है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 10]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/10" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عبداللہ بن عمرو رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: مسلمان وہ ہے جس کی زبان اور ہاتھ سے دوسرے مسلمان محفوظ رہیں، اور مہاجر وہ ہے جو ان چیزوں کو چھوڑ دے جن سے اللہ نے منع کیا ہے۔</p>

## Narration 11

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A11)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic dialogue. Attribution: Muhammad. Abu Musa reports a direct question to Allah&#x27;s Messenger about the best Islam and his answer about tongue and hand.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ الْقُرَشِيِّ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَبِي قَالَ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بُرْدَةَ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بُرْدَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي بُرْدَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي مُوسَى ـ رضى الله عنه ـ قَالَ قَالُوا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَىُّ الإِسْلاَمِ أَفْضَلُ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ سَلِمَ الْمُسْلِمُونَ مِنْ لِسَانِهِ وَيَدِهِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 11]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/11" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic dialogue. Attribution: Muhammad. Abu Musa reports a direct question to Allah&#x27;s Messenger about the best Islam and his answer about tongue and hand.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Musa: Some people asked Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), &quot;Whose Islam is the best? i.e. (Who is a very good Muslim)?&quot; He replied, &quot;One who avoids harming the Muslims with his tongue and hands</p>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अबू मूसा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि लोगों ने अर्ज़ किया: ऐ अल्लाह के रसूल! इस्लाम में कौन सा अमल अफ़ज़ल है? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़र्माया: वह जिसकी ज़बान और हाथ से मुसलमान महफ़ूज़ रहें।</p>

## Narration 12

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A12)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَمْرُو بْنُ خَالِدٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا اللَّيْثُ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ، عَنْ أَبِي الْخَيْرِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو ـ رضى الله عنهما ـ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، سَأَلَ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَىُّ الإِسْلاَمِ خَيْرٌ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ تُطْعِمُ الطَّعَامَ، وَتَقْرَأُ السَّلاَمَ عَلَى مَنْ عَرَفْتَ وَمَنْ لَمْ تَعْرِفْ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 12]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/12" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated &#x27;Abdullah bin &#x27;Amr: A man asked the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) , &quot;What sort of deeds or (what qualities of) Islam are good?&quot; The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) replied, &#x27;To feed (the poor) and greet those whom you know and those whom you do not Know (See Hadith No)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 12]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/12" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अब्दुल्लाह बिन अम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) से रिवायत है कि एक शख्स ने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा कि इस्लाम में कौन सा काम बेहतर है? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: खाना खिलाना और हर जाने वाले और अनजान को सलाम करना।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 12]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/12" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عبداللہ بن عمرو رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھا کہ اسلام میں کون سا کام بہتر ہے؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: کھانا کھلانا اور ہر جاننے والے اور انجان کو سلام کرنا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 12]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/12" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 13

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A13)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم‏.‏ وَعَنْ حُسَيْنٍ الْمُعَلِّمِ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا قَتَادَةُ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لا يُؤْمِنُ أَحَدُكُمْ حَتَّى يُحِبَّ لأَخِيهِ مَا يُحِبُّ لِنَفْسِهِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 13]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/13" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

## Narration 14

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A14)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو الْيَمَانِ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنَا شُعَيْبٌ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ فَوَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لاَ يُؤْمِنُ أَحَدُكُمْ حَتَّى أَكُونَ أَحَبَّ إِلَيْهِ مِنْ وَالِدِهِ وَوَلَدِهِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 14]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/14" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Huraira: &quot;Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;By Him in Whose Hands my life is, none of you will have faith till he loves me more than his father and his children</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 14]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/14" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: उस ज़ात की क़सम जिसके हाथ में मेरी जान है, तुममें से कोई शख़्स उस वक़्त तक मोमिन नहीं हो सकता जब तक मैं उसे उसके वालिद और औलाद से ज़्यादा महबूब न हो जाऊँ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 14]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/14" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اس ذات کی قسم جس کے ہاتھ میں میری جان ہے، تم میں سے کوئی شخص اس وقت تک مومن نہیں ہو سکتا جب تک میں اسے اس کے والد اور اولاد سے زیادہ محبوب نہ ہو جاؤں۔</p>

## Narration 15

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A15)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَعْقُوبُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ عُلَيَّةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ صُهَيْبٍ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ح وَحَدَّثَنَا آدَمُ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، قَالَ قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يُؤْمِنُ أَحَدُكُمْ حَتَّى أَكُونَ أَحَبَّ إِلَيْهِ مِنْ وَالِدِهِ وَوَلَدِهِ وَالنَّاسِ أَجْمَعِينَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 15]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/15" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت انس رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: تم میں سے کوئی شخص اس وقت تک مومن نہیں ہو سکتا جب تک میں اسے اس کے والد، اس کی اولاد اور تمام لوگوں سے زیادہ محبوب نہ ہو جاؤں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 15]</p>

## Narration 16

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A16)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الْمُثَنَّى، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ الثَّقَفِيُّ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَيُّوبُ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلاَبَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ ثَلاَثٌ مَنْ كُنَّ فِيهِ وَجَدَ حَلاَوَةَ الإِيمَانِ أَنْ يَكُونَ اللَّهُ وَرَسُولُهُ أَحَبَّ إِلَيْهِ مِمَّا سِوَاهُمَا، وَأَنْ يُحِبَّ الْمَرْءَ لاَ يُحِبُّهُ إِلاَّ لِلَّهِ، وَأَنْ يَكْرَهَ أَنْ يَعُودَ فِي الْكُفْرِ كَمَا يَكْرَهُ أَنْ يُقْذَفَ فِي النَّارِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 16]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/16" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   16 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Anas: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Whoever possesses the following three qualities will have the sweetness (delight) of faith: 1. The one to whom Allah and His Apostle become dearer than anything else. 2. Who loves a person and he loves him only for Allah&#x27;s sake. 3. Who hates to revert to Atheism (disbelief) as he hates to be thrown into the fire</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 16]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/16" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 16</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   16 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अनस (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जिसमें तीन बातें होंगी वह ईमान की मिठास पा लेगा: (एक यह कि) अल्लाह और उसका रसूल उसे सबसे ज़्यादा महबूब हों, (दूसरे यह कि) वह किसी इंसान से सिर्फ़ अल्लाह के लिए मुहब्बत करे, (तीसरे यह कि) वह कुफ़्र की तरफ़ लौटने को ऐसा बुरा समझे जैसे आग में डाले जाने को बुरा समझता है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 16]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/16" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت انس رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: جس میں تین باتیں ہوں گی وہ ایمان کی مٹھاس پا لے گا: (ایک یہ کہ) اللہ اور اس کا رسول اسے سب سے زیادہ محبوب ہوں، (دوسرے یہ کہ) وہ کسی انسان سے صرف اللہ کے لیے محبت کرے، (تیسرے یہ کہ) وہ کفر کی طرف لوٹنے کو ایسا برا سمجھے جیسے آگ میں ڈالے جانے کو برا سمجھتا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 16]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/16" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 17

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A17)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو الْوَلِيدِ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ جَبْرٍ، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ أَنَسًا، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ آيَةُ الإِيمَانِ حُبُّ الأَنْصَارِ، وَآيَةُ النِّفَاقِ بُغْضُ الأَنْصَارِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 17]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/17" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 17</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   17 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Anas: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Love for the Ansar is a sign of faith and hatred for the Ansar is a sign of hypocrisy</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 17]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/17" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 17</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अनस (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अंसार से मोहब्बत ईमान की निशानी है और अंसार से बग़ज़ निफ़ाक़ की निशानी है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 17]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Anas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ansaar se mohabbat imaan ki nishani hai aur Ansaar se bughz nifaaq ki nishani hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 17]</p>

## Narration 18

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A18)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو الْيَمَانِ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنَا شُعَيْبٌ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي أَبُو إِدْرِيسَ، عَائِذُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ أَنَّ عُبَادَةَ بْنَ الصَّامِتِ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ وَكَانَ شَهِدَ بَدْرًا، وَهُوَ أَحَدُ النُّقَبَاءِ لَيْلَةَ الْعَقَبَةِ ـ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ وَحَوْلَهُ عِصَابَةٌ مِنْ أَصْحَابِهِ ‏ &quot;‏ بَايِعُونِي عَلَى أَنْ لاَ تُشْرِكُوا بِاللَّهِ شَيْئًا، وَلاَ تَسْرِقُوا، وَلاَ تَزْنُوا، وَلاَ تَقْتُلُوا أَوْلاَدَكُمْ، وَلاَ تَأْتُوا بِبُهْتَانٍ تَفْتَرُونَهُ بَيْنَ أَيْدِيكُمْ وَأَرْجُلِكُمْ، وَلاَ تَعْصُوا فِي مَعْرُوفٍ، فَمَنْ وَفَى مِنْكُمْ فَأَجْرُهُ عَلَى اللَّهِ، وَمَنْ أَصَابَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ شَيْئًا فَعُوقِبَ فِي الدُّنْيَا فَهُوَ كَفَّارَةٌ لَهُ، وَمَنْ أَصَابَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ شَيْئًا ثُمَّ سَتَرَهُ اللَّهُ، فَهُوَ إِلَى اللَّهِ إِنْ شَاءَ عَفَا عَنْهُ، وَإِنْ شَاءَ عَاقَبَهُ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ فَبَايَعْنَاهُ عَلَى ذَلِكَ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 18]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/18" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated &#x27;Ubada bin As-Samit: who took part in the battle of Badr and was a Naqib (a person heading a group of six persons), on the night of Al-&#x27;Aqaba pledge: Allah&#x27;s Apostle said while a group of his companions were around him, &quot;Swear allegiance to me for: 1. Not to join anything in worship along with Allah. 2. Not to steal. 3. Not to commit illegal sexual intercourse. 4. Not to kill your children. 5. Not to accuse an innocent person (to spread such an accusation among people). 6. Not to be disobedient (when ordered) to do good deed.&quot; The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) added: &quot;Whoever among you fulfills his pledge will be rewarded by Allah. And whoever indulges in any one of them (except the ascription of partners to Allah) and gets the punishment in this world, that punishment will be an expiation for that sin. And if one indulges in any of them, and Allah conceals his sin, it is up to Him to forgive or punish him (in the Hereafter).&quot; &#x27;Ubada bin As-Samit added: &quot;So we swore allegiance for these.&quot; (points to Allah&#x27;s Apostle)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 18]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/18" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत उबादा बिन सामित (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अपने सहाबा की एक जमाअत के दरम्यान फ़रमाया: मुझसे बैअत करो कि अल्लाह के साथ किसी को शरीक नहीं ठहराओगे, चोरी नहीं करोगे, ज़िना नहीं करोगे, अपनी औलाद को क़त्ल नहीं करोगे, किसी पर बोहतान नहीं बांधोगे और किसी नेक काम में नाफ़रमानी नहीं करोगे। तुम में से जो इसे पूरा करेगा उस का अज्र अल्लाह के ज़िम्मे है, और जो इन में से किसी का इरतेक़ाब करे और इसे दुनिया में सज़ा मिल जाए तो वो उस के लिए कफ़्फ़ारा है, और अगर अल्लाह ने उस के गुनाह को छुपा लिया तो मामला अल्लाह के सुपुर्द है, चाहे तो माफ़ कर दे और चाहे तो सज़ा दे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 18]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/18" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عبادہ بن صامت رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنے صحابہ کی ایک جماعت کے درمیان فرمایا: مجھ سے بیعت کرو کہ اللہ کے ساتھ کسی کو شریک نہیں ٹھہراؤ گے، چوری نہیں کرو گے، زنا نہیں کرو گے، اپنی اولاد کو قتل نہیں کرو گے، کسی پر بہتان نہیں باندھو گے اور کسی نیک کام میں نافرمانی نہیں کرو گے۔ تم میں سے جو اسے پورا کرے گا اس کا اجر اللہ کے ذمہ ہے، اور جو ان میں سے کسی کا ارتکاب کرے اور اسے دنیا میں سزا مل جائے تو وہ اس کے لیے کفارہ ہے، اور اگر اللہ نے اس کے گناہ کو چھپا لیا تو معاملہ اللہ کے سپرد ہے، چاہے تو معاف کر دے اور چاہے تو سزا دے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 18]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/18" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ubadah bin Samit (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apne sahaba ki ek jama&#x27;at ke darmiyan farmaya: Mujh se bai&#x27;at karo ke Allah ke saath kisi ko shareek nahi thehraoge, chori nahi karo ge, zina nahi karo ge, apni aulaad ko qatl nahi karo ge, kisi par bohtan nahi bandho ge aur kisi nek kaam mein nafarmani nahi karo ge. Tum mein se jo ise poora kare ga us ka ajr Allah ke zimme hai, aur jo in mein se kisi ka irtekab kare aur ise duniya mein saza mil jaye toh woh us ke liye kaffara hai, aur agar Allah ne us ke gunah ko chhupa liya toh ma&#x27;amla Allah ke supurd hai, chahe toh maaf kar de aur chahe toh saza de.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 18]</p>

## Narration 19

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A19)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ أَبِي صَعْصَعَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ يُوشِكُ أَنْ يَكُونَ خَيْرَ مَالِ الْمُسْلِمِ غَنَمٌ يَتْبَعُ بِهَا شَعَفَ الْجِبَالِ وَمَوَاقِعَ الْقَطْرِ، يَفِرُّ بِدِينِهِ مِنَ الْفِتَنِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 19]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/19" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Said Al-Khudri: Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;A time will soon come when the best property of a Muslim will be sheep which he will take on the top of mountains and the places of rainfall (valleys) so as to flee with his religion from afflictions</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 19]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/19" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अबू सईद ख़ुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़र्माया: अनक़रीब एक ऐसा वक़्त आएगा कि मुसलमान का बेहतरीन माल बकरियां होंगी जिन्हें लेकर वह पहाड़ों की चोटियों और बारिश के मक़ामात पर चला जाएगा ताकि फ़ितनों से अपने दीन को बचा सके।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 19]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/19" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابوسعید خدری رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: عنقریب ایک ایسا وقت آئے گا کہ مسلمان کا بہترین مال بکریاں ہوں گی جنہیں لے کر وہ پہاڑوں کی چوٹیوں اور بارش کے مقامات پر چلا جائے گا تاکہ فتنوں سے اپنے دین کو بچا سکے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 19]</p>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Anqareeb ek aisa waqt aayega ke musalman ka behtareen maal bakriyan hon gi jinhein le kar woh pahadon ki chotiyon aur baarish ke maqamaat par chala jayega taake fitnon se apne deen ko bacha sake.</p>

## Narration 20

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A20)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدَةُ، عَنْ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا أَمَرَهُمْ أَمَرَهُمْ مِنَ الأَعْمَالِ بِمَا يُطِيقُونَ قَالُوا إِنَّا لَسْنَا كَهَيْئَتِكَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، إِنَّ اللَّهَ قَدْ غَفَرَ لَكَ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِكَ وَمَا تَأَخَّرَ‏.‏ فَيَغْضَبُ حَتَّى يُعْرَفَ الْغَضَبُ فِي وَجْهِهِ ثُمَّ يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّ أَتْقَاكُمْ وَأَعْلَمَكُمْ بِاللَّهِ أَنَا ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 20]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/20" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated &#x27;Aisha: Whenever Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) ordered the Muslims to do something, he used to order them deeds which were easy for them to do, (according to their strength and endurance). They said, &quot;O Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)! We are not like you. Allah has forgiven your past and future sins.&quot; So Allah&#x27;s Apostle became angry and it was apparent on his face. He said, &quot;I am the most Allah fearing, and know Allah better than all of you do</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 20]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/20" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   20 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है कि रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जब लोगों को कोई हुक्म देते तो ऐसा काम करने का हुक्म देते जिसकी उनमें ताक़त होती। लोगों ने कहा: ऐ अल्लाह के रसूल! हम आप जैसे नहीं हैं, अल्लाह ने आपके अगले-पिछले तमाम गुनाह माफ़ कर दिए हैं। यह सुनकर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ग़ुस्सा हो गए और आपके चेहरे पर ग़ुस्सा नुमायाँ हो गया, फिर फ़र्माया: मैं तुम सब से ज़्यादा अल्लाह से डरने वाला और उसे जानने वाला हूँ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 20]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/20" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم جب لوگوں کو کوئی حکم دیتے تو ایسا کام کرنے کا حکم دیتے جس کی ان میں طاقت ہوتی۔ لوگوں نے کہا: اے اللہ کے رسول! ہم آپ جیسے نہیں ہیں، اللہ نے آپ کے اگلے پچھلے تمام گناہ معاف کر دیے ہیں۔ یہ سن کر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم غصہ ہو گئے اور آپ کے چہرے پر غصہ نمایاں ہو گیا، پھر فرمایا: میں تم سب سے زیادہ اللہ سے ڈرنے والا اور اسے جاننے والا ہوں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 20]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/20" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jab logon ko koi hukam dete to aisa kaam karne ka hukam dete jis ki un mein taaqat hoti. Logon ne kaha: Aey Allah ke Rasool! Hum aap jaise nahin hain, Allah ne aap ke agle pichhle tamaam gunaah maaf kar diye hain. Yeh sun kar aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) gussa ho gaye aur aap ke chehre par gussa numayan ho gaya, phir farmaya: Mein tum sab se ziyada Allah se darne wala aur use jaane wala hoon.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 20]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/20" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 21

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A21)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ حَرْبٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ ثَلاَثٌ مَنْ كُنَّ فِيهِ وَجَدَ حَلاَوَةَ الإِيمَانِ مَنْ كَانَ اللَّهُ وَرَسُولُهُ أَحَبَّ إِلَيْهِ مِمَّا سِوَاهُمَا، وَمَنْ أَحَبَّ عَبْدًا لاَ يُحِبُّهُ إِلاَّ لِلَّهِ، وَمَنْ يَكْرَهُ أَنْ يَعُودَ فِي الْكُفْرِ بَعْدَ إِذْ أَنْقَذَهُ اللَّهُ، كَمَا يَكْرَهُ أَنْ يُلْقَى فِي النَّارِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 21]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/21" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 21</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   21 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Anas: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Whoever possesses the following three qualities will taste the sweetness of faith: 1. The one to whom Allah and His Apostle become dearer than anything else. 2. Who loves a person and he loves him only for Allah&#x27;s sake. 3. Who hates to revert to disbelief (Atheism) after Allah has brought (saved) him out from it, as he hates to be thrown in fire</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 21]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/21" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 21</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   21 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अनस (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तीन चीज़ें ऐसी हैं कि जिस में वो होंगी, वो ईमान की मिठास पा लेगा: (1) यह कि अल्लाह और उसका रसूल उसे हर चीज़ से ज़्यादा महबूब हो जाएँ। (2) यह कि वो किसी शख़्स से सिर्फ़ अल्लाह ही के लिए मोहब्बत करे। (3) यह कि वो कुफ़्र में वापस लौटने को ऐसा बुरा समझे जैसा कि आग में डाले जाने को बुरा समझता है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 21]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/21" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت انس رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: تین چیزیں ایسی ہیں کہ جس میں وہ ہوں گی، وہ ایمان کی مٹھاس پا لے گا: (1) یہ کہ اللہ اور اس کا رسول اسے ہر چیز سے زیادہ محبوب ہو جائیں۔ (2) یہ کہ وہ کسی شخص سے صرف اللہ ہی کے لیے محبت کرے۔ (3) یہ کہ وہ کفر میں واپس لوٹنے کو ایسا برا سمجھے جیسا کہ آگ میں ڈالے جانے کو برا سمجھتا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 21]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/21" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Anas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Teen cheezein aisi hain ke jis mein woh hoongi, woh iman ki mithas pa le ga: (1) Yeh ke Allah aur Us ka Rasool use har cheez se zyada mahboob ho jayein. (2) Yeh ke woh kisi shakhs se sirf Allah hi ke liye muhabbat kare. (3) Yeh ke woh kufr mein wapas lautne ko aisa bura samjhe jaisa ke aag mein dale jane ko bura samajhta hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 21]</p>

## Narration 22

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A22)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا إِسْمَاعِيلُ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ يَحْيَى الْمَازِنِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، رضى الله عنه ـ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ يَدْخُلُ أَهْلُ الْجَنَّةِ الْجَنَّةَ، وَأَهْلُ النَّارِ النَّارَ، ثُمَّ يَقُولُ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى أَخْرِجُوا مَنْ كَانَ فِي قَلْبِهِ مِثْقَالُ حَبَّةٍ مِنْ خَرْدَلٍ مِنْ إِيمَانٍ‏.‏ فَيُخْرَجُونَ مِنْهَا قَدِ اسْوَدُّوا فَيُلْقَوْنَ فِي نَهَرِ الْحَيَا ـ أَوِ الْحَيَاةِ، شَكَّ مَالِكٌ ـ فَيَنْبُتُونَ كَمَا تَنْبُتُ الْحِبَّةُ فِي جَانِبِ السَّيْلِ، أَلَمْ تَرَ أَنَّهَا تَخْرُجُ صَفْرَاءَ مُلْتَوِيَةً ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ قَالَ وُهَيْبٌ حَدَّثَنَا عَمْرٌو ‏&quot;‏ الْحَيَاةِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ وَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ خَرْدَلٍ مِنْ خَيْرٍ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 22]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/22" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Said Al-Khudri: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;When the people of Paradise will enter Paradise and the people of Hell will go to Hell, Allah will order those who have had faith equal to the weight of a grain of mustard seed to be taken out from Hell. So they will be taken out but (by then) they will be blackened (charred). Then they will be put in the river of Haya&#x27; (rain) or Hayat (life) (the Narrator is in doubt as to which is the right term), and they will revive like a grain that grows near the bank of a flood channel. Don&#x27;t you see that it comes out yellow and twisted?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 22]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/22" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से इस्माइल ने यह हैदिथ बयां की अपने बाप से रिवायत करते हैं और वह अबू सईद खुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) और वह नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से नक़ल करते हैं के आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया जब जन्नती जन्नत में और दोज़खी दोज़ख में दाखिल हो जायेंगे. अल्लाह पाक फ़रमायेगा निकल लो. तब (ऐसे लोग) दोज़ख से निकाल लिए जायेंगे और वह जल कर कोयले की तरह सियाह हो चुके होंगे. फिर ज़िन्दगी की नहर में या बारिश के पानी में डाले जायेंगे. (यहाँ रवि को शक हो गया है के ऊपर के रवि ने कौन सा लफ्ज इस्तेमाल किया) उस वक़्त वह दाने की तरह उग आएंगे जिस तरह नदी के किनारे दाने उग आते हैं. क्या तुम ने नहीं देखा के दाने ज़र्दी मेल पेच दर पेच निकलता है. वाहिब ने कहा के हम से अम्र ने (&#x27;हाय&#x27; की बजाये) &#x27;हयात&#x27; मं ईमान&#x27;) की बजाये (&#x27;खर्ड़ल मं खैर&#x27;) का लफ़्ज़ बयां किया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 22]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/22" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابوسعید خدری رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: جنتی جنت میں اور جہنمی جہنم میں داخل ہو جائیں گے، پھر اللہ تعالیٰ فرمائے گا: جس کے دل میں رائی کے دانے کے برابر بھی ایمان ہو اسے نکال لو۔ چنانچہ وہ نکالے جائیں گے اور وہ جل کر کوئلہ ہو چکے ہوں گے۔ پھر انہیں &#x27;نہر حیا&#x27; (یا حیات) میں ڈالا جائے گا، (راوی کو شک ہے) تو وہ اس طرح اگیں گے جیسے سیلاب کے کنارے دانہ اگتا ہے۔ کیا تم نے نہیں دیکھا کہ وہ زرد اور مڑا ہوا نکلتا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 22]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/22" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Ismail ne yeh hadith bayan ki, woh kehte hain unse Malik ne, woh Amr bin Yahya al-Mazni se naqal karte hain, woh apne baap se riwayat karte hain aur woh Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) aur woh Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se naqal karte hain ke Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya jab Jannati Jannat mein aur Dozakhi Dozakh mein dakhil ho jayenge. Allah Pak farmayega, jis ke dil mein rai ke daane ke barabar (bhi) imaan ho, usko bhi dozakh se nikal lo. Tab (aise log) dozakh se nikal liye jayenge aur woh jal kar koyle ki tarah siyah ho chuke honge. Phir zindagi ki nehar mein ya barish ke pani mein dale jayenge. (Yahan rawi ko shak ho gaya hai ke upar ke rawi ne kaun sa lafz istemal kiya) us waqt woh daane ki tarah ug aayenge jis tarah nadi ke kinare daane ug aate hain. Kya tum ne nahi dekha ke daana zardi mail pech dar pech nikalta hai. Waheeb ne kaha ke hum se Amr ne (&#x27;Haya&#x27; ki bajaye) &#x27;Hayat&#x27;, aur (&#x27;Khardal min imaan&#x27;) ki bajaye (&#x27;Khardal min khair&#x27;) ka lafz bayan kiya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 22]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/22" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 23

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A23)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ صَالِحٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي أُمَامَةَ بْنِ سَهْلٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ أَبَا سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيَّ، يَقُولُ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ بَيْنَا أَنَا نَائِمٌ رَأَيْتُ النَّاسَ يُعْرَضُونَ عَلَىَّ، وَعَلَيْهِمْ قُمُصٌ مِنْهَا مَا يَبْلُغُ الثُّدِيَّ، وَمِنْهَا مَا دُونَ ذَلِكَ، وَعُرِضَ عَلَىَّ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَعَلَيْهِ قَمِيصٌ يَجُرُّهُ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ قَالُوا فَمَا أَوَّلْتَ ذَلِكَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ الدِّينَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 23]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/23" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Said Al-Khudri: Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;While I was sleeping I saw (in a dream) some people wearing shirts of which some were reaching up to the breasts only while others were even shorter than that. Umar bin Al-Khattab was shown wearing a shirt that he was dragging.&quot; The people asked, &quot;How did you interpret it? (What is its interpretation) O Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)?&quot; He (the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) ) replied, &quot;It is the Religion</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 23]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/23" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अबू सईद ख़ुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मैं सो रहा था कि मैंने लोगों को अपने सामने पेश होते देखा, उनमें से कुछ ने क़मीसे पहन रखी थीं जो सीने तक पहुँचती थीं, और कुछ इससे भी छोटी थीं। फिर उमर बिन ख़त्ताब को पेश किया गया और उन्होंने ऐसी क़मीस पहन रखी थी जिसे वह घसीट रहे थे। लोगों ने पूछा: ऐ अल्लाह के रसूल! आपने इसकी क्या ताबीर ली? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: दीन।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 23]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/23" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابوسعید خدری رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: میں سو رہا تھا کہ میں نے لوگوں کو اپنے سامنے پیش ہوتے دیکھا، ان میں سے کچھ نے قمیصیں پہن رکھی تھیں جو سینے تک پہنچتی تھیں، اور کچھ اس سے بھی چھوٹی تھیں۔ پھر عمر بن خطاب کو پیش کیا گیا اور انہوں نے ایسی قمیص پہن رکھی تھی جسے وہ گھسیٹ رہے تھے۔ لوگوں نے پوچھا: اے اللہ کے رسول! آپ نے اس کی کیا تعبیر لی؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: دین۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 23]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/23" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Abu Sa&#x27;eed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Main so raha tha ke main ne logon ko apne samne pesh hote dekha, un mein se kuch ne qameesein pehan rakhi theen jo seene tak pahunchti theen, aur kuch is se bhi chhoti theen. Phir Umar bin Khattab ko pesh kiya gaya aur unhon ne aisi qamees pehan rakhi thi jise woh ghaseet rahe the. Logon ne poocha: Ae Allah ke Rasool! Aap ne is ki kya tabeer li? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Deen.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 23]</p>

## Narration 24

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A24)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ يُوسُفَ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكُ بْنُ أَنَسٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَرَّ عَلَى رَجُلٍ مِنَ الأَنْصَارِ وَهُوَ يَعِظُ أَخَاهُ فِي الْحَيَاءِ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ دَعْهُ فَإِنَّ الْحَيَاءَ مِنَ الإِيمَانِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 24]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/24" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 24</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   24 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated &#x27;Abdullah (bin &#x27;Umar): Once Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) passed by an Ansari (man) who was admonishing his brother regarding Haya&#x27;. On that Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Leave him as Haya&#x27; is a part of faith.&quot; (See Hadith)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 24]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/24" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 24</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   24 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन यूसुफ़ ने हम से बयान किया, वह कहते हैं के हमें मालिक बिन अनस ने इब्न-ए-शिहाब से ख़बर दी, वह सालिम बिन अब्दुल्लाह से नक़ल करते हैं, वह अपने बाप (अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा)) से के एक दफ़ा रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) एक अन्सारी शख़्स के पास से गुज़रे इस हाल में के वह अपने एक भाई से कह रहे थे के तुम इतनी शर्म क्यों करते हो। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उस अन्सारी से फरमाया के इसको इसके हाल पर रहने दो क्यूंके हया भी ईमान ही का एक हिस्सा है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 24]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/24" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ایک انصاری کے پاس سے گزرے جو اپنے بھائی کو حیاء (شرم و حیا) کے بارے میں نصیحت کر رہا تھا۔ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اسے چھوڑ دو، کیونکہ حیاء ایمان کا حصہ ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 24]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/24" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Yusuf ne hum se bayan kiya, woh kehte hain ke hamein Malik bin Anas ne Ibn-e-Shihab se khabar di, woh Salim bin Abdullah se naqal karte hain, woh apne baap (Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma)) se ke ek dafa Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ek Ansari shakhs ke paas se guzre is haal mein ke woh apne ek bhai se keh rahe the ke tum itni sharm kyun karte ho. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne us Ansari se farmaya ke isko iske haal par rehne do kyunke haya bhi imaan hi ka ek hissa hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 24]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/24" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 25

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A25)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Belief</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ الْمُسْنَدِيُّ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو رَوْحٍ الْحَرَمِيُّ بْنُ عُمَارَةَ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ وَاقِدِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ أَبِي يُحَدِّثُ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أُمِرْتُ أَنْ أُقَاتِلَ النَّاسَ حَتَّى يَشْهَدُوا أَنْ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَأَنَّ مُحَمَّدًا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ، وَيُقِيمُوا الصَّلاَةَ، وَيُؤْتُوا الزَّكَاةَ، فَإِذَا فَعَلُوا ذَلِكَ عَصَمُوا مِنِّي دِمَاءَهُمْ وَأَمْوَالَهُمْ إِلاَّ بِحَقِّ الإِسْلاَمِ، وَحِسَابُهُمْ عَلَى اللَّهِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 25]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/25" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Ibn &#x27;Umar: Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: &quot;I have been ordered (by Allah) to fight against the people until they testify that none has the right to be worshipped but Allah and that Muhammad is Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), and offer the prayers perfectly and give the obligatory charity, so if they perform that, then they save their lives and property from me except for Islamic laws and then their reckoning (accounts) will be done by Allah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 25]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/25" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इस हदीस को हम से अब्दुल्लाह बिन मुहम्मद मुसनदी ने बयान किया, उनसे अबू रूह हरमी बिन अमारा ने, उनसे शुअबा ने, वह वाक़िद बिन मुहम्मद से रिवायत करते हैं, वह कहते हैं मैं ने यह हदीस अपने बाप से सुनी, वह इब्न-ए-उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) से रिवायत करते हैं के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, मुझे (अल्लाह की तरफ़ से) हुकुम दिया गया है के लोगों से जंग करूँ उस वक़्त तक के वह इस बात का इक़रार कर लें के अल्लाह के सिवा कोई माबूद नहीं है और यह के मुहम्मद (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अल्लाह के सच्चे रसूल हैं और नमाज़ अदा करने लगें और ज़कात दें, जिस वक़्त वह यह करने लगेंगे तो मुझ से अपने जान-ओ-माल को महफ़ूज़ कर लेंगे, सिवाये इस्लाम के हक़ के। (रहा उनके दिल का हाल तो) उनका हिसाब अल्लाह के ज़िम्मे है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 25]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/25" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابن عمر رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: مجھے حکم دیا گیا ہے کہ میں لوگوں سے لڑوں یہاں تک کہ وہ گواہی دیں کہ اللہ کے سوا کوئی معبود نہیں اور محمد اللہ کے رسول ہیں، اور نماز قائم کریں اور زکوٰۃ ادا کریں۔ جب وہ یہ کر لیں گے تو انہوں نے مجھ سے اپنے خون اور مال کو محفوظ کر لیا، سوائے اسلام کے حق کے، اور ان کا حساب اللہ کے سپرد ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 25]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/25" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Is hadith ko hum se Abdullah bin Muhammad Musnadi ne bayan kiya, unse Abu Rooh Harmi bin Amara ne, unse Shuba ne, woh Waqid bin Muhammad se riwayat karte hain, woh kehte hain main ne yeh hadith apne baap se suni, woh Ibn-e-Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) se riwayat karte hain ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, mujhe (Allah ki taraf se) hukum diya gaya hai ke logon se jang karoon us waqt tak ke woh is baat ka iqrar kar lein ke Allah ke siwa koi mabood nahi hai aur yeh ke Muhammad (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Allah ke sachay Rasool hain aur namaz ada karne lagein aur zakat dein, jis waqt woh yeh karne lagenge to mujh se apne jaan-o-maal ko mehfooz kar lenge, siwaye Islam ke haq ke. (Raha unke dil ka haal to) unka hisab Allah ke zimme hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 25]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/25" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
